**KOSHISH #10 - Engle-Granger Cointegration (Engle & Granger 1987, Nobel 2003) - PEHLE SE LIKHA QAIDA (13 Oct, user 'D', naap se PEHLE, badalna mana):** `indicators/jas_coint_radar.pine` c1, TradingView chart OANDA:XAUUSD 1D, 4 kitaabi jodiyan (pehle se chuni): Gold/Silver, Gold/Platinum, WTI/Brent, AUD/NZD. Har din pichhle 250 din par ln(A) = a + b ln(B) regression -> residual e, sigma, z. Entry: khali + b > 0 + 2 <= |z| < 4 + ADF t (e par, bina constant/lag) <= -3.34; z >= +2 = A SELL + B BUY (b se), ulta bhi; entry par a/b/sigma fix. Bahar: fix z 0 ke paar / |z| >= 4 ulta (SL) / 60 din. 1R = 2 sigma, kharcha 0.005% har leg har taraf. TALAASH (entry 2000-2018) PASS = kul trades >= 50 AUR >= 3/4 musbat AUR mila Exp - 2SE > 0. IMTIHAN (2019+) chhupa; TALAASH PASS par: mila Exp > 0 AUR >= 3/4 musbat AUR kul >= 20 = ASLI PASS. Table 'ADF juri' = |z| >= 2 wale dinon mein jori kitni dafa juri thi (maloomat). Claude umeed: FAIL (ADF trades kam kar deta hai). **NATEEJA (13 Oct, c1 compile ✅, user screenshot) = TALAASH FAIL (trades bohot kam):** Gold/Silver 4 trades -0.564R, ADF juri 4/644 (1%) · Gold/Platinum 5 -0.187, 5/428 (1%) · WTI/Brent 6 +0.581, 6/413 (1%) · AUD/NZD 1 -1.114, 1/585 (0%); kul 16 trades, musbat 1/4, mila -0.051 (±0.592). Abhi: b 0.38 / 0.49 / 1.00 / 0.95 (WTI/Brent 1.00, AUD/NZD 0.95 = engine samajhdar). User ne ghalti ka shak kiya -> Claude ne ADF hisaab dobara jaancha (Δe = γ e[-1], SSR = Σde² - γΣe·de, t = γ/se) - ghalti nahi mili. **Wajah:** 250 din par ADF ki taqat kam - residual ki aadhi-umar ~3 hafte ho to t ~ -1.9, -3.34 tak sirf bohot tez wapsi (~1 hafta) pohanchti hai. Matlab: sakht test ke hisaab se ye jodiyan 1 saal ke daur mein taqreeban kabhi 'juri' sabit nahi hotin. **Cointegration (ADF wala) band.** **KOSHISH #11 (13 Oct, user 'D') - PEHLE SE LIKHA QAIDA:** wahi `jas_coint_radar.pine`, build **c2**: ADF shart BAND (switch `useAdf`, default false) = Gatev, Goetzmann, Rouwenhorst (2006) jaisa bina-test pairs trading (asal Gatev normalized qeemat ka faasla leta tha; yahan wahi 250 din regression residual). Jodiyan, z 2/0/4, 60 din, 1R, kharcha, TALAASH/IMTIHAN shartein BILKUL c1 wali. FAIL = jori wali trading POORI band. Claude umeed: FAIL (ADF ne bataya rishta aksar kamzor). **NATEEJA (13 Oct, c2 compile ✅, user screenshot) = TALAASH FAIL:** Gold/Silver 73 trades -0.044 (±0.213) · Gold/Platinum 48 -0.054 (±0.254) · WTI/Brent 56 +0.086 (±0.264) · AUD/NZD 45 -0.052 (±0.246); kul 222, musbat 1/4, mila **-0.015R (±0.121)**, Exp - 2SE -0.136. Bina test bhi sifar ke aas paas = ADF ne sahi bataya tha (rishta wapsi ke qabil nahi). **JORI WALI TRADING POORI BAND** (pehle se likha).

MILA HUA: **49 ishare** (>= 30 ✓), pehle paar **32.7%** vs aam din 43.6% (636), **FARK -3.3% ±12.1** (FARK - 2SE < 0 ✗). Upar wapsi (neeche laal) 15: 66.7% vs 66.1% (-3.3 ±23.7); neeche wapsi (upar hari) 34: 17.6% vs 23.7% (-3.4 ±14.0). Tokri: 2-3 ATR 7 · 28.6% vs 40.9%; 3-4 18 · 33.3% vs 38.7%; 4-6 12 · 41.7% vs 49.1%; 6+ 12 · 25.0% vs 29.5%. 40 din mein faisla nahi: ishara 44, aam 367. Pakar ~17%. **Matlab:** 49 dafa shakal bani, sirf ~16 dafa qeemat palat kar numayan candle paar gayi, ~33 dafa pehle utna hi aur aage gayi - 'hamesha' wala dawa naap mein ~1/3 nikla, aam din se bhi kam (magar farq shor ke andar). Yaad mein jo 'neeche wali candle' aati hai woh baad mein chuni hui hoti hai (jo kaam kar gayi); us waqt pata nahi hota ke yeh aakhri hai. Swing Fail jaisa: bari chaal aksar aage jaati hai. Trade idea nahi; settings ghumana mana.
- **NUMAYAN GINTI n1.1 (12 Oct raat) - user ne Claude ki samajh par 'D' diya** (aur kaha: Order Block / Fractal / LG jaisi cheezein bilkul nahi jaanta). `indicators/jas_numayan_ginti.pine` + `tools/numayan_check.py`. Trade nahi, sirf ginti. **PEHLE SE LIKHA QAIDA (gold par chalne se PEHLE, badalna mana):** numayan = body >= 1 ATR(14); U = taza numayan hari (60 din, high na chhua ho); ISHARA = kal aur aaj close U ke low ke neeche + aaj numayan laal; AAM = wohi haalat, aaj numayan laal nahi; 40 din ki DAUD: U ka high pehle = jeet, utna hi faasla neeche pehle = haar (ek din dono = haar); ulta bhi. FARK = ishara % - aam % (side x faasla tokri). OANDA:XAUUSD 1D 2000 se: ishara >= 30 AUR FARK - 2SE > 0 = ISHARA ASLI; < 30 = KAM WAQIAT; warna SABIT NAHI. **Pakar pehle se maloom:** ek gold series par ~40 ishare, 2SE ~15% -> sirf bara faida (~20%+) pakra jata hai; SABIT NAHI = 'bara faida nahi mila', shakal jhooti nahi. Python (random): FARK ~0, jhoota ASLI ~5%. Jaanch workflow (4 agent): compile ghalti nahi, Pine = Python, aage dekhna nahi; durust kiya: jo laal U ko khud toorti thi (59% ishare) woh 'girawat' hai -> sirf pehle se neeche wali laal; side x tokri alag (drift jhukao); chart ka asal pehla saal table mein. (Nateeja upar: SABIT NAHI.)
- **DURUSTI (12 Oct raat, user):** user ka nishan FVG tha hi NAHI - Claude ne ghalat samjha. User ka asal mushahida: girawat se pehle upar ek NUMAYAN (bari) hari candle; neeche ek numayan laal candle chhor kar qeemat palat-ti hai aur upar wali numayan candle ko paar (cross) karti hai (SMC zabaan mein 'Order Block' jaisa). FVG ka nateeja sirf FVG ka hai, user ke idea ka nahi. Agla: Claude ki samajh user se 'D' ho, phir pehle se likha qaida + ginti script.
- **FVG v1 NATEEJA (12 Oct, OANDA:XAUUSD 1D, 2000 se, 20 din, user screenshot) = ITTEFAQ:** compile ✅. MILA HUA: **1539 FVG din** (>= 200 ✓), **FARK -4.5% ±3.8** (FARK - 2SE < 0 ✗). Tokri: 0-0.5 ATR 89.9% vs aam 94.1%; 0.5-1 78.4/81.8; 1-1.5 65.3/67.0; 1.5-2.5 51.0/61.4; 2.5+ 23.8/42.9. - **Rukh score -100..+100:** bara structure pivot 3 (35, rukh QILA ke paar band candle par palta - qila = toote swing high ke baad ka low), MA21/63 (20), Supertrend 10/3 (15), ADX >= 18 (10), bari TF (20; auto 1m->15m, 3-15m->1H, 30m/1H->4H, 2-4H->1D). Haal: TEZ UPAR >= 70, UPAR >= 30, RANGE, NEECHE <= -30, TEZ NEECHE <= -70.
- **Advance ishara ▲:** chhota structure (pivot 2) neeche se UPAR palta (andar ka CHOCH) + bari TF NEECHE nahi + toorne wali hari candle body >= 0.5 ATR ya FVG. Ek palte par ek. Band candle, repaint nahi. Alert bhi.
- **Pehle se likha qaida (10 Oct, ishare dekhne se PEHLE, badalna mana; number code mein constant):** har ishare ke baad 2 ATR pehle (jeet) ya 1 ATR ulta (haar), ek candle mein dono = haar, 100 candle = gina nahi; buniyad = har aam candle usi taraf. **100+ ishare aur FARK > 2SE = KAAM KA -> strategy copy + Tester; 100+ aur FARK <= 2SE = SABIT NAHI (sirf dekhna); kam = GINTI KAM.** Settings badli to table faisla nahi deta. Pehle XAUUSD 15m, phir 1H.

Python port `tools/trend_check.py` (random qeemat 12 x 5000): pehli shakal mein qila kabhi qeemat ke ghalat taraf banta tha (purana toota swing low yaad rehta tha) -> theek (pullLo). **k4 ke qaide (user ne khud diye, 3-4 Oct) - inhi par chalna, dobara mat poochna:**
- **Q0:** koi akeli lot ya jori **nuqsan par band nahi** - sirf 0 ya faida. Har sawal ka woh jawab chuno jo "0 ya faida" de ("asal maqsad profit").
- **Q1** rukh (H1 EMA50) ke khilaf kabhi nahi ("trend is our friend"). **Q2** rukh ke saath har step ek 0.01 (pehle faide wali ulti 0.01 band, warna nayi). **Q3** NET hadd 0.05. **Q4** lot kabhi 0.01 se bari nahi.
- **Q5** hedge (NET 0) fauran: chot se 1 step wapsi, rukh badle, news se pehle, Jumma; phir 300 sec thehrao. **Q6** bari lot ki jori Close By, sab se faide wali, >= 0. **Q7** seconds ka hisaab (panel + CSV `MQL5\Files\JasChakkar_<symbol>.csv`). **Q8** equity attach-waqt ki 95% rok / 90% hedge + ruk.
- **Q9 Close All** (user ka sab se purana qaida): symbol ki SAARI lots mila kar `InpCloseAll` (+30 demo) faide mein -> sab band -> rukh dekh kar naya setup. User demo par haath se 274 aur 182 lots par Close All kar chuka (+30.16 USD).

**Sabaq jo naape gaye (dobara mat sikhana):** chakkar asal mein 0.01 ki rukh wali shart hai (k1 gold demo $1 step: 47 chakkar, 20/27, -6.85, gold gira); $1 step par spread+slippage ~15%, $5 par ~3%. k3 mein Close All na hone se hedge har dafa nayi lots jorta tha (kitab 270+).

**SMC v4 (7 Oct raat) - BAN GAYA, compile baqi:** `indicators/smc_coach_pro_v4.pine` (v3 waisi hi rakhi hai, wapas jana ho to). (1) `//@version=6`. (2) Durust ki gayi kharabiyan: a) 4H/1D rukh band candle par lookahead_off + [1] tha - history par ek HTF candle zyada purana, live par sahi (expectancy ghalat) -> lookahead_on + [1]; b) DXY ka apna TF ho to wahi -> band candle + lookahead_on; c) ek swing ko dobara chhoone par BOS dobara + usi jagah NAYA zone -> ek swing ek rukh mein ek dafa (beech mein CHOCH ho to phir ginta hai); d) TP1 room sirf aakhri ulte zone se -> sab ulte zones mein sab se qareeb kinara (MTF signal par bhi); e) purani virtual trade band hone par naye signal ka naqsha bhi mit jata tha -> `planBar` se sirf apna; f) 1000+ candle purane swing ka wick parhna (max_bars_back) -> hifazat; g) v6 lazy and/or/ternary: ST ATR, naali MA, DXY EMA, naali crossover, session ab har candle par. (3) **PIP HISAAB** (naya, sab se neeche, input group 15): `request.security_lower_tf` 1m se 1D aur 1H candle ka RAASTA (Upar / Neeche / Kul raasta / Asal farq / Kaam % / Range, pip mein; gold 1 pip = 0.10, baqi mintick x10, input se badlo) + PROFILE (har pip ke khane mein minute ya volume, POC, VA 70%). Table "PIP HISAAB" (rows: 1D abhi, 1D kal, 1H abhi, 1H pichla; columns ke tooltip mein $ qeemat), chart par 1D (ya 1H) profile ki pattiyan + POC/VAH/VAL lakeerein - aakhri candle se aage kabhi nahi. Sirf aakhri 4 din gine jate hain (TradingView ki ~100k andar ki candles ki hadd). Profile 3000 khane se zyada ho to khana dugna (BTC). Python port se naapa: Upar - Neeche = Asal farq (200 din, farq 0), minute ka jor wahi, VA >= 70%. `tools/pinecheck.py` ab `method` pehchanta hai; v4 par "No structural issues". **Signal ke qawaid nahi badle** (sirf upar wali durustiyan) - is liye v3 se signal thore kam/alag ho sakte hain (duplicate BOS/zone hate).

**(purana) Kaam jo user ne 7 Oct ko manga tha:** "SMC Pro v2 jaisa naam, ~1530 lines" wali Pine script ko **`//@version=6`** mein karna, aur ho sake to behtar (tweak). Repo mein qareeb tareen: `indicators/smc_coach_pro_v3.pine` (pehle naam smc_coach_pro_v2, **1625 lines, abhi @version=5**). User se poochha gaya: yahi file hai ya us ke computer ki koi aur (to paste kare)? - **file ka jawab baqi** (nayi chat mein pehle yahi poochho). User ki do shartein (7 Oct): (1) **ek bhi kharabi na rahe**; (2) **"1H ya 1D TF mein jitne bhi pips hote hain, har pip ka hisaab script mein rakhe"** - matlab poochha gaya (candle ke andar upar/neeche ka poora raasta pips mein? har price level par waqt/volume (profile)? ya sirf range?) - **user ne "dono" kaha = raasta (upar/neeche pips, asal farq) + price profile (har qeemat par waqt/volume)**; is ke liye `request.security_lower_tf` (1m/5m candles) lagega, 1D par 1m ki had ~ chand din. v6 mein badalte waqt dhyan: `na` ko bool mein nahi rakh sakte, `int`/`float` ka khud badalna kam, `when=` hata, `transp` hata, `security` lookahead, `strategy` ke parameters; repo mein `tools/pinecheck.py` hai.

## SMC Coach Pro v3 (indicators/smc_coach_pro_v3.pine) - COMPILE HO GAYA, chal raha hai (2 Oct)

- 2 Oct dopahar ke baad ye badla: daen taraf pressure ek line; naali default band; labels/plan sirf 1m; legend 4 row neeche.
- **MTF 90% signal (naya, compile baqi):** 1m-1W sab TF ka ausat pressure 90%+ ek taraf, har TF usi taraf, 1m candle usi taraf band, close MA21 ke sahi taraf, ulta zone TP1 se pehle na ho -> 1m par Entry/SL/TP1/TP2. TP1 par aadhi + BE. Apni alag expectancy (daen table, "MTF 90%:" line). Bari candles ka high/low 1m candles se joda jata hai (history = live). User sirf TEST kar raha hai, trade nahi.

- **15m signal (naya, compile baqi, 2 Oct):** user ne 15m wala naqsha manzoor kiya. 15m chart par: 15m/1H/4H/1D ka ausat pressure 80%+, chaaron usi taraf, 1H MA21/63 usi taraf (band candle), 15m candle usi taraf band, MA21 ke sahi taraf magar 1.5 ATR se zyada door nahi (peecha nahi), ulta zone TP1 se pehle nahi. SL/TP1(1R, aadhi+BE)/TP2(2R), 64 candle timeout. Daen table 15m par bhi; baen legend sirf 1m.

### Purana (v3 pehli shakal)

- Neeche wala poora naqsha file mein kar diya. **Abhi tak compile nahi hua** - user 1m XAU chart par lagaye.
- Pressure (daen) = close candle ki range mein kahan hai (low = 0% BUY, high = 100% BUY), har TF ki chalti candle.
- MA21/63 EMA hain (signals bhi inhi se chalte hain - pehle 20/50 the).
- Naali (low/high channel) aur us ke teer nahi chhede - naqshe mein zikr nahi tha. User chahe to band.
- Expectancy line: sirf BAND virtual trades, R fi trade, qaus mein ginti.

### Manzoor naqsha (2 Oct)

1. Saari tafseel (tables/likhai) **sirf 1m chart par**. Baqi TF par koi table/likhai nahi.
2. TF list: **1m, 5m, 15m, 30m, 1H, 3H, 4H, 1D, 1W** (3m aur 2H nahi, 3H shamil).
3. Baen (legend): har TF ki **chalti candle** - O/H/L/C, upar/neeche, kitni bhari, **FVG/LGB**, **SZ/BZ**.
4. Daen: har TF ki chalti candle ka **BUY% / SELL% pressure**.
5. Hatana: "BUY/SELL missing" list, HTF line, **virtual tracker** (aur us ka expectancy panel), chart par baqi likhai.
6. **MA21 / MA63**: har chart par us chart ke APNE TF ki do lakeerein (1m par doosre TF ki MA nahi). Purani EMA20/50 + band ki jagah 21/63.
7. Chart ke nishan (zone dabbe, BUY/SELL teer, SL/TP, pivot lines) **rehne dein**.
8. User ne **B** chuna: tracker andar chupa chalta rahe (koi panel/teer nahi), sirf **ek chhoti line: BUY expectancy / SELL expectancy**.

## 6. RUKA HUA (user ne yaad mein rakha, 14 Oct): JasIbsEA i3

**Kyun:** i2 Tester ki 8 trades TradingView (`strategies/jas_ibs_test.pine`) se milayin: 6/8 signal
din same; 6 mili trades TV -0.07R vs MT5 -0.93R. Engine ki ghalti NAHI. Wajah:
1. **Candle ka waqt:** OANDA (TV) daily candle 17:00 New York par band, Exness 00:00 GMT (20:00 NY).
2. **Jumma:** Pine Jumma close par trade karta hai, EA Itwar raat (weekend gap: 24 Jul +46, 11 Sep -42).

**Naqsha:**
- EA apni daily candle H1 (ya M1) se banaye, **17:00 NY par band** (US DST khud - `UsDst` /
  `InpServerGMT` code `JasImomEA.mq5` se copy).
- Faisla 17:00 NY se **~5 minute pehle**, Jumma ko bhi. Tick na aaye (chhutti) to bazaar khulne par
  mukammal candle se (i2 jaisa).
- Baqi WAHI: IBS < 0.2 + close > SMA200 = BUY; band IBS > 0.8 ya 5 din; SL 3 ATR(14) RMA; 1%;
  SL wale din nayi nahi; magic 20261022; Journal TRADE lines (i2).
- **Jaanch:** Tester US500m Daily, 2015.01.01 - aaj, 1 minute OHLC, 10000 USD. Signal din TV se
  BILKUL milein: 13 Jul, 20 Jul, 24 Jul, 05 Aug, 14 Aug, 28 Aug, 04 Sep, 23 Sep, 30 Sep.

**Koshish #36 qaida (29 Oct, 26vi chat, user ki ChatGPT Pine "HTF + AOI + M30 Engulfing" ka ASAL idea, Claude ki tareef; test se PEHLE, badalna mana) - `tools/aoi_engulf_test.py`:**

**Rukh:** pichhli BAND W / D / 4H candle (D = 17:00 NY, W = Jumma 17:00 NY, 4H NY 17:00 se jura) ka close vs us TF ka EMA50: 3 mein >= 2 upar = sirf BUY,
>= 2 neeche = sirf SELL. **AOI:** pichhle 90 din ki 4H pivot high/low (5 dono taraf, band hone ke baad maloom); satah "mazboot" = 0.5 x ATR14(D) ke andar
kam az kam 2 pivot. Zone = satah +- 0.25 x ATR(D). **Entry:** M30 band engulfing: BUY = pichhli candle laal, ye hari, O <= pichhla C aur C >= pichhla O,
body > pichhli body; engulfing ka low zone ko chhoye (low <= satah + 0.25A), close zone ke upar ya andar (C >= satah - 0.25A), satah <= C. SELL ulta.
Agli M30 open par. **SL** = engulfing low - 0.1 x ATR14(M30) (SELL: high + ...), **TP** = 2R. M1 par jaanch, ek M1 mein dono = SL, gap = open. 240 M30 (5 din)
= agli open band. Ek waqt ek position. Kharcha max($0.50, 0.03%) + swap 3%/saal har raat -> R.
**TALAASH 2021-24:** trades >= 100, Exp(R) - 2SE > 0, saal >= 3/4 musbat. BUY/SELL alag + bina kharcha = maloomat. **IMTIHAN** 2025+ ek dafa (M1 user se),
Exp > 0 + trades >= 30; gold 2025 tezi -> BUY/SELL alag dekho. Ginti +1. (27vi chat ka noise-boundary ab #37.)

**Koshish #37 qaida (29 Oct, 27vi chat, Zarattini-Aziz-Barbon 2024 "Beat the Market" noise-boundary intraday momentum, paper jaisa; test se PEHLE, badalna mana) - `tools/noise_boundary_test.py`:**
USATECH + USA500, QDM M1 2021-24 (`data/qdm/`), UTC -> NY. Sirf cash session 09:30-16:00 NY; din ka Open = 09:30 M1 open, kal ka Close = 15:59 M1 close.
Din jis mein 09:30 ya 15:59 bar na ho / session mein < 300 M1 = chhor do (half-day bhi). **Noise:** har minute t (09:30 se) ke liye
sigma(t) = pichhle 14 din ka ausat |Close(t) / Open(din) - 1| (aaj shamil nahi). **Hadd:** UB = max(Open, kal Close) x (1 + sigma), LB = min(Open, kal Close) x (1 - sigma).
**Entry:** sirf :00 aur :30 par (10:00 se 15:30 tak), us minute ka close > UB = BUY, < LB = SELL, usi close par. **Stop (trailing):** BUY = max(UB, VWAP), SELL = min(LB, VWAP),
sirf :00 / :30 ke close par jaancho (paper jaisa, beech mein nahi). Stop lage to usi din agle :00 / :30 signal par dobara entry jaiz. **16:00 = sab band** (raat nahi, swap nahi).
VWAP = aaj 09:30 se M1 (H+L+C)/3 x volume; volume 0 ho to bina volume ausat. Ek market ek position, grid / martingale / hedge nahi.
**R** = sigma(t) x entry (entry waqt ka noise faasla). Kharcha 0.03%/taraf (`ftmo_test.py` COST) -> R. Paper ka vol-target lot = sirf maloomat (% munafa bhi likho).
**TALAASH 2021-24 (TOKRI = dono market saath):** trades >= 100, Exp(R) - 2SE > 0, saal >= 3/4 musbat. Har market alag, BUY/SELL alag, bina kharcha = maloomat.
Ek market chuna to IMTIHAN sakht (Exp - 2SE > 0, pakki 4c). **IMTIHAN** 2025+ ek dafa (M1 user se), Exp > 0 + trades >= 30. Andaza ~15-25 tr/mahina/market. Ginti +1.

**Koshish #39 qaida (29 Oct, 27vi chat, user ki TV built-in "Momentum strategy" Pine, length 12, H1; test se PEHLE, badalna mana) - `tools/mom_h1_test.py`:**
TOKRI XAUUSD, XAGUSD, US500, USTEC, Dukascopy H1 (`data/ftmo/`, UTC), #34 jaisa. mom0 = C - C[12], mom1 = mom0 - mom0[1].
Band candle par mom0 > 0 aur mom1 > 0 = agli candle ke liye BUY STOP high + 1 tick; mom0 < 0 aur mom1 < 0 = SELL STOP low - 1 tick; warna order cancel.
Fill = max(open, stop) (SELL: min). Ulta fill = palat (purani band + nayi khuli, hedge nahi); usi taraf dobara = kuch nahi (pyramiding 1). SL / TP nahi.
**R** = ATR14(H1) entry waqt (unit). Kharcha 0.03% x (entry+exit), gold min $0.50; swap 3%/saal har raat. Trade = entry se palat tak.
**TALAASH entry < 2019:** trades >= 100, Exp - 2SE > 0, market musbat >= 3/4, (market x saal) > 50% musbat. Bina kharcha = maloomat.
**IMTIHAN 2019+** ek dafa: Exp - 2SE > 0, trades >= 30. TV ka nateeja (kharcha 0, ek market, dikha kar chuna) = sirf ishara. Ginti +1.

**Koshish #38 qaida (29 Oct, 27vi chat, Carmine Rosato draft Qaida 1 "failed breakdown" + Raschke Turtle Soup, US index; aankre draft ki table se; test se PEHLE, badalna mana) - `tools/fail_break_test.py`:**

**Waqt:** sirf woh H1 candle jo 09:00, 10:00, 11:00 NY par shuru ho (NY open ke ~2 ghante). **BUY:** is candle samet pichhli 3 candle ka sab se neecha low <= L - 0.1 ATR
aur ye candle L ke UPAR band (wapsi). **SELL ulta:** 3 candle ka high >= H + 0.1 ATR aur close H ke neeche. Din mein har market ki sirf pehli signal; agli candle open par entry.

Ek market ek position. Kharcha 0.03% x (entry+exit) -> R.

**Koshish #43 qaida (29 Oct, 28vi chat, user "D", test se PEHLE, badalna mana) - J Law "Multiple Edge" (Chart Fanatics, 25 Oct 2026) - `tools/jlaw_mea_test.py`:**

Naap (har TF par): ATR14 (RMA), SMA20, EMA20, VOL20 = pichhle 20 bar ka ausat volume (B-1 tak). Swing high = high[k] = max(high[k-3..k+3]), pakka k+3 par.
(1) **Momentum toot (bar B):** L = B-1 tak aakhri pakka swing high; close[B] > L, close[B-1] <= L, close[B] > open[B], (H-L)[B] >= 1.5 x ATR[B-1], vol[B] >= 1.5 x VOL20[B-1].
(2) **Intezar** B+1..B+15; close < L - 1.0 ATR = setup khatam; naya toot = naya setup.
(3) **Pullback / entry bar i:** low[i] <= L + 0.5 ATR[i]; |EMA20[i] - L| <= 1.0 ATR[i] (level + MA ek jagah); close[i] >= L - 0.5 ATR[i];
**kam volume:** B+1..i ka ausat volume < VOL20[B-1]. Entry = open[i+1]. **SL** = min(L, low[i]) - 0.5 ATR[i] (entry <= SL = chhor do). 1R = entry - SL.
(4) **Exit:** SL (low <= SL -> SL par, gap neeche = open); ya close[j] < SMA20[j] - 0.5 ATR[j] -> open[j+1] ("sell into weakness"). TP nahi.
Kharcha #34 jaisa: 0.03% x (entry+exit), gold kam az kam $0.50, swap 3%/saal har raat. R = (exit - entry - kharcha) / 1R.
**TALAASH** entry < 2019 (index data 2012 se): har TF alag PASS = kul >= 100 tr, Exp(R) - 2SE > 0, >= 3/4 market Exp > 0, (market x saal) musbat > 50%. Ginti +2.
**IMTIHAN** 2019+ ek dafa, bina tabdeeli: tr >= 30 aur Exp > 0; dono mein se ek TF chuna = sakht (Exp - 2SE > 0). Earnings / sector / RS wala hissa CFD par nahi (sirf chart).
**Koshish #43 nateeja (29 Oct, 28vi chat) = #43a + #43b TALAASH FAIL** (IMTIHAN nahi khola). #43a daily: 3 tr (toot 58 range wale, volume 1.5x sirf 5 - CFD volume) = ginti kam.
#43b 4H: 293 tr, Exp -0.115R, SE 0.119, Exp-2SE -0.353, jeet 26%, market 1/4 (sirf USTEC +0.26R, 33 tr), saal 4/16; bina kharcha +0.031R (~0).
Sabaq: J Law ka asal faida stocks ke catalyst / sector / RS mein hai; sirf chart wala hissa (toot + kam volume pullback) 4 CFD par edge nahi.

**Koshish #44 qaida (29 Oct, 28vi chat, user ne video di, test se PEHLE, badalna mana) - JJ Simon "Fair Pricing Theory" 1m NASDAQ (Chart Fanatics) - `tools/jj_fair_price_test.py`:**
Data `data/ustec/USTEC_M1_2021-2022_dukascopy.zip` (UTC, Bid) = sab TALAASH; IMTIHAN = USATECH M1 2025+ (laptop JAS_DATA_BG) aane par EK dafa. Sirf USTEC (wo sirf NQ).
**Session** (NY waqt, DST ke saath), har ek 90 min: Asia 18:00, NY 09:30, NY PM 14:00. **FP** (fair price) = session ki pehli 1m candle ki open. News wala hissa nahi (calendar nahi).
**A. Continuation** (session mein ek): pehli candle; rang = sign(close-open); bias = pichhle 6 ghante ki chaal ulti (open se 6h pehle ki open -> pehli candle se pichhli close:
upar = SHORT bias). Rang == bias, aur close pichhli 3 candles ke low se neeche (SELL) / high se upar (BUY) -> entry agli candle ki open. TP 38 / SL 25 point; pehli candle ka (H-L) > 25 = TP 76 / SL 50.
**B. Reversion FP ki taraf** (A ke baad / A na ho): candle i (session ke 90 min ke andar) close FP se upar = SELL dhoondo, neeche = BUY. Signal (koi ek), SELL (BUY ulta):
(1) displacement: i laal, i-1 hari, body[i] > body[i-1], close[i] < low[i-1]; (2) BOS: aakhri na-toota swing low (low[j] < low[j-1] aur < low[j+1], i-1 tak pakka) ke neeche pehli close.
Shart: |close[i] - FP| >= 0.8 x 38 = 30.4 point. Entry open[i+1], TP 38 / SL 25 (static, TP FP par band nahi). Ek waqt ek position.
**Exit:** TP / SL M1 high-low se (ek hi candle mein dono = SL); 60 candle mein na lage = 60vi ki close. Session mein **reversion ki lagataar 3 haar = session band**.
**Kharcha** (PASS isi par): 3.0 point har trade (spread ~1.5-2 + slippage; Bid data). Maloomat: 0 kharcha aur 0.03%/taraf (purana sakht). R = (point - kharcha) / SL.
**PASS (TALAASH 2021-22):** >= 100 tr, Exp(R) - 2SE > 0, dono saal musbat, A aur B alag bhi dikhao (sirf ek PASS = sirf woh IMTIHAN, sakht). Ginti +1.
**Koshish #44 nateeja (29 Oct, 28vi chat) = TALAASH FAIL** (IMTIHAN band). 2491 tr (**103.8/mahina**), Exp -0.135R, SE 0.023, Exp-2SE -0.182, jeet 39.6% (38/25 ka
barabar = 39.7%), 2021 -146R, 2022 -190R. A 432 tr -0.082R, B 2059 tr -0.146R; Asia -0.13, NY -0.18, NYPM -0.04; BUY/SELL dono manfi. Bina kharcha -0.018R (~0), 0.03% -0.33R.
Sabaq: 1m "fair price" bias = sikka (jeet bilkul tukka jitni); JJ khud kehta hai faida prop-firm ke hisaab (sasti eval, 45 account, payout) se, market se "slightly +EV". 100 tr/mahina mil gaye magar edge nahi.

**Koshish #46 qaida (29 Oct, 28vi chat, user "D", test se PEHLE, badalna mana) - Bollinger Squeeze + Crabel NR7 toot (user ki PDF "9 Advanced", #7 + #8) - `tools/squeeze_nr7_test.py`:**
Data `data/ftmo/` H1 -> **#46a daily** + **#46b 4H** (NY 17:00). 4 market XAU, XAG, US500, USTEC. BUY + SELL (alag bhi dikhao), har market ek position.
**Setup bar i:** NR7 = (H-L)[i] pichhle 7 bar (i samet) mein sab se kam; aur Squeeze = BW[i] = (upper-lower)/SMA20 (Bollinger 20, 2) pichhle 125 bar ke **neeche ke 20%** mein (percentile <= 20).
**Entry:** i+1..i+5 mein pehla bar jo high[i] se upar jaye = BUY (stop high[i], gap = open) / low[i] se neeche = SELL. Ek hi bar mein dono = BUY/SELL jo open ke qareeb, aur usi bar SL = -1R. Naya setup purana khatam.
**SL** = doosri taraf: BUY low[i] - 0.1 ATR14, SELL high[i] + 0.1 ATR14. 1R = |entry - SL|. **Exit:** close SMA20 ke ulti taraf (BUY: close < SMA20) -> open agla; ya SL. TP nahi.
Kharcha #34 jaisa (0.03% x (entry+exit), gold min $0.50, swap 3%/saal). **TALAASH** entry < 2019: har TF PASS = kul >= 100 tr, Exp(R) - 2SE > 0, >= 3/4 market Exp > 0,
(market x saal) musbat > 50%. Ginti +2. **IMTIHAN** 2019+ ek dafa: tr >= 30, Exp > 0; ek hi TF PASS = sakht (Exp - 2SE > 0).
**Koshish #46 nateeja (29 Oct, 28vi chat) = #46a + #46b TALAASH FAIL** (IMTIHAN band). #46a daily: 305 tr, Exp +0.313R magar SE 0.253 (chhota NR7 SL = kuch bari jeet), Exp-2SE -0.192,
jeet 28%, market 3/4, (market x saal) 20/45, saal 9/16. #46b 4H: 1553 tr, Exp -0.194R, Exp-2SE -0.389, jeet 18%, 1/4, 10/46; bina kharcha +0.09R. BUY/SELL dono kamzor.
Sabaq: "shant ke baad toot" = tukka; daily ka + sirf chand bari trades se (bharosa nahi).
